## 1301B

- **record** — entire record _(open identity flag: Catalog code '1301B' was not found on any source. The record follows MASIMO MS-1301BG, the black glass-door unit in the 1301 series. MS-1301 is a white solid-door unit with different dimensions and volume. QA 2026-11-11: Home Center (s6) sells Westin WES-1301B, a black solid-door office fridge (94 L gross / 91 L net, 832 × 442 × 475 mm, same as the white MS-1301), next to WES-1301BG (black vitrine). So '1301B' may be a black solid-door unit, which would match the catalog's 91 L but not its 'שקוף ויטרינה'. If the store's unit has a solid door, this record's dimensions, volume, shelves and content are wrong for it. Check the box label or the supplier invoice to confirm the store's unit is MS-1301BG before QA passes. Do not change the catalog model_number (join key).)_
